Title: De patriarchis Nestorianorum commentaria, ex codicibus Vaticanis edidit Henricus Gismondi S.J. Pars altera: Amri et Slibae textus (archive.org catalogue title 'Amri et Slibae de Patriarchis Nestorianorum / Akhbar batarikat kursi al-Mashriq')
Creator: Amr ibn Matta of Tirhan (fourteenth century) and Sliba ibn Yuhanna of Mosul; Enrico (Henricus) Gismondi S.J. (ed.)
Publisher: Romae: excudebat F. de Luigi (file line 71); printed by the Tipografia della Casa Editrice Italiana, Roma 1896 (file line 78)
Publication Date: 1896 (file line 78; the title-page year is truncated to 'XCVI' in the OCR at file line 72)
Rights: Public Domain (pd-us-by-date: published 1896); the item record carries no rights field
Language: ara (the Arabic text recovered, about 102,000 Arabic characters, with Gismondi's Latin title page and preface ('Lectori humanissimo salutem' at file line 82); the Arabic OCR has frequent errors and Latin-script intrusions, so quotations need the page image)
Source: archive.org item amrietslibaedepa0000henr (Beth Mardutho: The Syriac Institute; sponsor Henry Luce Foundation), full text at https://archive.org/download/amrietslibaedepa0000henr/amrietslibaedepa0000henr_djvu.txt. Fetched directly 2026-10-17 by the Shared-Events source-research thread (Era 2 worker C); the byte count of the downloaded file (285,553) equals the size archive.org lists for the djvu.txt.
Provenance note: Extracted unaltered: the item's own djvu.txt is reproduced whole after this header; nothing was stripped, normalized or corrected. Rights basis: an 1896 Rome printing is out of US copyright under this project's pd-us-by-date rule.
Content note: The Arabic text of Amr and Sliba's lives of the patriarchs of the East. For 410: Marutha the bishop carrying the letter to Yazdgard, who rejoices and answers it, and the gathering of forty bishops and metropolitans in the eleventh year of Yazdgard's reign at file lines 4813 to 4818; the see vacant for a year after Isaac at 4788; Yazdgard at 4061, 4705 and 4734. The only original-language witness in this directory for the Arabic patriarchal histories; a fourteenth-century memory witness, never evidence of the synods on its own. The Latin version of the same text is in the companion pars altera file.

o s quae de Patriarchis Nestorianorum Mares filius 
Salomonis suo libro T'urris intexuit Amrum Matthaei tirhanen- 
sem retraetasse, nec non ilud Amri opus Slibam Iohannis mossu- 
lensem ei coaevum paulo aliter digessisse, recepere qui rei eccle- 
sasticae orientalium historiam vestigaverunt. Opus quod Amro 
tribuitur exhibet vaticanus codex inter arabieos CX pervetustus, 
foliis perperam ac temere assutis, qui utpote emendationibus notu- 
lisque eodem calamo exaratis passim conspersus, autographi potius 
quam transcripti exemplaris speciem refert. Uberiorem Slibae recen- 
sionem codex alius continet, iam pridem ad bibliothecam Neophyto- 
rum s. Mariae ad Montes de Urbe pértinens, aliquot abhinc annis 


inter vatic. arab. (Neoph. XLI) adnumeratus('). Hoc is incipit initio :

Borgiano Ser. K. VI, vol. 14: id usui mihi fuit ad scripta quandoque incerta 
rite legenda.

Utriusque textus collatio idem prorsus opus prodit, quum res 
eaedem iisdem fere continenter vocabulis expressae in utroque de- 
prehendantur, praeter pauca quae a Sliba addita dicenda sunt, nisi 
quis verius duxerit Amrum quae in Slibae opere superflua videban- 
tur resecuisse. Hinc licuit utramque uno conspectu elucubrationem 
exhibere. Slibae nempe textum protuli, quae ei prae Amro pro- 
pria sunt uncinis inclusa repraesentans, opus tamen Amro inscripsi 
auctori scilicet primo, ut fert opinio: cuius propria aliquot, a quibus 
discrepat Sliba, seorsim collecta in appendice recensui. A differen- 
tiis aliis indicandis abstinui quae in usu synonymorum consistunt, 
vel in diverso ordine recensendi quae apud utrumque auctorem 
eadem sunt, ut alicuius Primatis anni episcopatus aut vitae, dies 
obitus, locus sepulturae et similia: vocalia signa subinde adieci 
ad sensus perspicuitatem. Dictionis soloecismos, quorum complures 
ad vulgare eius temporis eloquium scriptionemque pertinent, ut in 
textu. sunt, retinui, nisi nimiae quandoque offensioni lectori essent, 
ut quae emendavi pag. 1, lin. 1: T3] الواححد بعد‎ , et lin. 3: 
MAL Gs بد عظيم‎ aliaque huiusmodi haud multa quae libra- 
rio potius indocto quam scriptori nostro sat erudito imputanda 


videntur.

Opus Maris ex codice vaticano CIX cuius lacunas ex codice 
parisiensi (Bibliothecae national. CXC supplere licuit, brevi, Deo 
favente, me editurum confido, tunc forte prolaturus si quid trium 
lucubrationum penitior inspectio suggesserit quod redigat ad verum 
de Amro ac Sliba dubitationem, uter opus alterius fecerit suum. 

Gratias habeo quam plurimas cl. Ignatio Guidi qui summa qua 


praestat humanitate perutili me iuvit opera in hac paranda editione.
